Native cheap path: Read-and-confirm immediately before a low-volume action or use immutable inputs instead of broad transactional machinery.</p></dd><dt>Case family ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>R2-C02</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Case coverage limit</dt><dd><p>Family-level finite probes, not individual empirical validation of every directional expansion. Native operands and guards own the full claim.</p></dd><dt>Cluster ids</dt><dd><ol></ol></dd><dt>Tension ids</dt><dd><ol></ol></dd><dt>Emergent parent ids</dt><dd><ol></ol></dd><dt>Evidence boundary</dt><dd><p>Exposed constructed analytical author contribution using exact qualified native records.
